The actual Grand Prix may not start until Saturday, but the action on a weekend like this most definitely begins on Friday! As usual, Friday is when players have the last chance to earn byes via one of the Grand Prix Trials. 32 players enter, but after five rounds of single-elimination carnage only one of them remains and gets to walk away with the top prize of two free wins in the weekend's main event.

All of the following decklists have been played to a 5-0 finish by their respective pilots. As such, they offer a first small glimpse of what we can expect at this Grand Prix. Check them out!

#### Alexey Romanchuk, Grand Prix Trial Winner
